### [1] [Synthetic Designed Experiments for Diagnosing Vision Model Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00832)
*Krisanu Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SDRS方法，利用实验设计统计学理论，将合成数据生成器视为实验装置，通过部分因子设计和ANOVA分解审计下游模型的因子敏感性，将失败分类为Type I（覆盖不足）和Type II（虚假依赖）两类，并针对性地生成合成数据。在三个实验中验证了该方法能有效识别模型缺陷并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据流水线采用开环范式，将合成数据视为廉价真实数据随机采样，未能利用合成数据可控、独立变化场景因子的独特特性，导致无法针对性地覆盖下游模型的失败模式。

Method: 基于实验设计统计理论，将下游模型视为黑箱系统、合成生成器视为实验装置。采用部分因子设计高效审计模型的因子敏感性（ANOVA分解），将失败分为Type I（因子水平覆盖不足）和Type II（虚假无关依赖）两类，并针对性生成合成数据进行纠正。

Result: (1) dSprites诊断实验：准确率从49.9%提升至79.0%；(2) 程序化场景分割任务：mIoU从0.948提升至0.998；(3) 纠缠检测实验：ANOVA审计能识别不完美生成器中的跨因子污染。此外发现因子级不变性惩罚可在因子间传递敏感性，指出了表征级校正的开放问题。

Conclusion: SDRS通过实验设计框架有效识别和弥补了模型的两种失败类型，验证了将合成数据生成从开环随机采样转向闭环定向诊断的优越性，同时揭示了因子间敏感性转移这一开放挑战。

Abstract: Current synthetic data pipelines for computer vision generate images without diagnosing what the downstream model actually needs. This open-loop paradigm treats synthetic data as cheap real data, randomly sampling the generator's output space and hoping to cover the model's failure modes. We argue this fundamentally misuses synthetic data's unique property: the controllable, independent variation of scene factors.Drawing on the statistical theory of Design of Experiments (DoE), we propose Synthetic Designed Experiments for Representational Sufficiency (SDRS). SDRS treats the downstream model as a black-box system and the synthetic generator as an experimental apparatus. Using fractional factorial designs, SDRS efficiently audits a model's factor-sensitivity profile via ANOVA decomposition. It classifies failures into two actionable types: Type I gaps (coverage failures on underrepresented factor levels) and Type II gaps (reliance on spurious nuisance dependencies). The audit then prescribes targeted synthetic data to address each gap type. We validate SDRS on three experiments: (1) a controlled diagnostic on dSprites with planted biases, where the audit correctly identifies both gap types and targeted data improves accuracy from 49.9% to 79.0%; (2) a dense segmentation task on procedural scenes, where detecting background-complexity shortcuts and applying targeted data improves mIoU from 0.948 to 0.998; and (3) an entanglement detection experiment showing that the ANOVA audit identifies cross-factor contamination in imperfect generators. Finally, we show that per-factor invariance penalties can transfer sensitivity between factors, identifying an open problem for representation-level correction.

</details>


### [2] [Latent Space Probing for Adult Content Detection in Video Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00874)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Chiquita Prabhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜空间探针的框架，通过在CogVideoX视频扩散模型的去噪潜变量上附加轻量级分类器，实现实时成人内容检测。在包含11039个视频片段的数据集上达到97.29%的F1分数，额外开销仅4-6ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频的色情内容检测方法仅针对文本提示或解码后的像素空间输出，忽视了生成过程中丰富的内部表示，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 在CogVideoX视频扩散模型推理过程中，拦截去噪后的潜空间表示，附加轻量级探针分类器进行实时检测；同时构建了包含11039个十秒视频片段的大规模二分类数据集。

Result: 在保留测试集上达到97.29%的F1分数，检测延迟仅4-6ms。

Conclusion: 潜空间信号编码了用于有害内容检测的强判别特征，相较于现有方法在检测性能和计算成本上均有改善。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of AI-powered video generation systems has introduced significant challenges in content moderation, particularly with respect to adult and sexually explicit material. Existing detection methods operate on either prompts or decoded pixel-space outputs. Therefore, both approaches are blind to the rich internal representations formed during generation. In this paper, we propose a novel latent space probing framework that intercepts the denoised latent representations produced by the CogVideoX video diffusion model during inference and attaches lightweight classifiers to perform real-time adult content detection. To support this work, we construct a large-scale binary dataset of 11039 ten-second video clips (5086 violating, 5953 non-violating) sourced from adult websites and YouTube respectively. We introduce two lightweight probing classifier architectures. We train and evaluate it on the dataset. Our work demonstrates that latent-space signals encode strong discriminative features for harmful content detection, achieving 97.29% F1 on our held-out test set with an overhead in the 4-6ms range. Our results suggest that probing the latent space results in improvements in both detection performance as well as cost.

</details>


### [3] [Single Image Defogging Using a Fourth-Order Telegraph PDE Guided by Physical Haze Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00878)
*Manish Kumar,Rajendra K. Ray*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合四阶非线性PDE与物理雾霾形成模型的混合图像去雾方法，通过暗通道先验估计大气参数并生成引导图像，利用四阶PDE（电报型方程）进行最终恢复，在保持结构细节的同时有效抑制雾霾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中图像去雾面临场景深度未知、大气散射以及缺乏真实参考图像等逆问题挑战，现有方法在去雾效果和结构保持方面存在不足。

Method: 1) 使用暗通道先验估计大气参数并生成引导图像；2) 构建融合物理雾霾形成模型的混合去雾框架；3) 采用四阶电报型PDE进行演化，包含边缘自适应扩散系数和由透射图加权的保真项；4) 使用相对误差范数准则判断PDE收敛。

Result: 与暗通道先验、改进暗通道先验及基于变分的单图像去雾方法相比，该方法提供了可比的视觉质量并保持了结构细节；有参考指标（MSE、SSIM）和无参考指标（FADE、对比度恢复指数、平均梯度、熵）均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 混合PDE方法能有效抑制雾霾同时保持结构细节，四阶扩散和双曲型公式改善了数值稳定性与收敛行为，为图像去雾提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In real-world scenarios, image defogging is an inverse problem due to unknown scene depth, atmospheric scattering, and the common absence of ground truth . To resolve the issue, we propose a hybrid defogging model that integrates a fourth-order nonlinear PDE with a physical haze formation model. We used Dark Channel Prior to estimate atmospheric parameters and to generate a guidance image, while the final restoration is performed via a fourth-order PDE-based evolution. A fourth-order PDE of the type telegraph is then evolved, incorporating an edge-adaptive diffusion coefficient and a fidelity term weighted by the transmission map. Fourth-order diffusion effectively suppresses haze while preserving structural details, and the hyperbolic formulation improves numerical stability and convergence behavior. We use relative error norm criteria for the convergence of our PDE. The proposed method is compared with Dark Channel prior, modified Dark Channel prior, and variational-based single-image defogging techniques. When we have ground truth available, we use MSE and SSIM for quantitative evaluation, whereas no-reference metrics, including FADE, Contrast Restoration Index, Average Gradient, and Entropy, are applied to real-world foggy images. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed hybrid PDE-based method provides comparable visual quality and maintains structural details.

</details>


### [4] [Intervention-Based Self-Supervised Learning: A Causal Probe Paradigm for Remote Photoplethysmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00882)
*Zhiyi Niu,Xiaoguang Tu,Bo Zhao,Junzhe Cao,Dan Guo,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出生理因果探测(PCP)范式，通过干预视频的色度分量来验证rPPG假设的物理真实性，从而避免自监督学习中的相关陷阱，显著提升模型在复杂场景下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督rPPG方法易陷入相关陷阱，学习到高频运动或光照噪声等主导周期信号，而非微弱的真实rPPG信号，导致模型泛化能力差。

Method: 提出PCP范式，核心是从被动相关学习转向主动精确干预：通过PhysMambaFormer提取rPPG假设，再利用可控生理信号编辑器在视频色度域进行精确干预，通过"归零可证伪性"和"公理等变性"验证假设的物理真实性。

Result: 在VIPL-HR和MMPD等挑战性数据集上，域内和跨域性能均有提升；在复杂跨数据集设置中超越有监督基线；在干净数据集上略有竞争性。

Conclusion: PCP范式有效抵抗运动和光照伪影，通过因果干预而非相关性学习，提升了rPPG模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Remote Photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables convenient non-contact physiological measurement. Existing Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) methods commonly fall into a correlation trap: they tend to learn the most dominant periodic signals in the data, such as high-energy motion or illumination noise, rather than the faint, true rPPG signal, leading to poor model generalization. To address this, we propose a new SSL paradigm, Physiological Causal Probing (PCP), which treats the latent rPPG signal as the underlying physical source and the resulting pixel chrominance variations as its visual manifestation. Its core idea is to shift from passive correlation learning to active, precise intervention: it intervenes on the video based on a proposed rPPG hypothesis, and verifies whether the post-intervention changes match physical expectations. We propose the Interv-rPPG framework to implement PCP: an rPPG extractor named PhysMambaFormer hypothesizes the rPPG signal, while a Controllable Physiological Signal Editor conducts precise chrominance-domain interventions on videos based on this hypothesis. Interv-rPPG validates the physical realism of the hypothesis through `Falsifiability via Nulling' and `Axiomatic Equivariance'. Our editor achieves precise editing of the rPPG signal by intervening in the low-frequency chrominance components of the video. Our method improves both in-domain and cross-domain performance on challenging datasets such as VIPL-HR and MMPD. Furthermore, it surpasses the supervised baseline in complex cross-dataset settings, while remaining competitive on clean datasets where the intervention mechanism may introduce slight residual chrominance noise. Extensive experiments, including diagnostic analysis of nuisance sensitivity, demonstrate that the PCP paradigm effectively resists motion and illumination artifacts.

</details>


### [5] [Towards High Fidelity Face Swapping: A Comprehensive Survey and New Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00883)
*Qi Li,Weining Wang,Shuangjun Du,Bo Peng,Jing Dong,Kun Wang,Zhenan Sun,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对换脸技术进行了全面的综述和基准测试，将现有方法组织为五大范式，并引入CASIA FaceSwapping基准数据集和标准化评估协议，通过大量实验揭示了当前技术的性能特征与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有换脸方法分散于不同范式，评估缺乏标准化数据集和协议，且已有综述主要关注更广泛的深度伪造生成或检测，换脸作为独立问题研究不足。

Method: 对现有方法进行结构化综述，归纳为五大范式并系统分析其设计原则、优势与局限；构建具有平衡人口分布和明确属性变化的CASIA FaceSwapping基准数据集，建立标准化评估协议。

Result: 对代表性方法进行了大量实验，获得了当前技术性能特征和局限性的新见解。

Conclusion: 本文提供了统一视角和原则性评估框架，以促进更鲁棒、可控的换脸方法的发展。

Abstract: Face swapping has witnessed significant progress in recent years, largely driven by advances in deep generative models such as GANs and diffusion models.Despite these advances, existing methods remain fragmented across different paradigms, and their evaluation is highly inconsistent due to the lack of standardized datasets and protocols. Moreover, prior surveys primarily focus on broader deepfake generation or detection, leaving face swapping insufficiently studied as a standalone problem. In this paper, we present a comprehensive survey and benchmark for face swapping. We provide a structured review of existing methods, organizing them into five major paradigms and systematically analyzing their design principles, strengths, and limitations. To enable fair and controlled evaluation, we introduce CASIA FaceSwapping, a high-quality benchmark with balanced demographic distributions and explicit attribute variations, and establish standardized protocols to assess the robustness of different face swapping methods. Extensive experiments on representative approaches yield new insights into the performance characteristics and limitations of current techniques. Overall, our work provides a unified perspective and a principled evaluation framework to facilitate the development of more robust and controllable face swapping methods. More results can be found at https://github.com/CASIA-NLPRAI/face-swapping-survey.

</details>


### [6] [LiteVLA-H: Dual-Rate Vision-Language-Action Inference for Onboard Aerial Guidance and Semantic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00884)
*Justn williams,Kishor Datta Gupta,Roy George,Mrinmoy Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiteVLA-H，一个256M参数的紧凑型VLA系统，在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上实现双速率操作：快速外环引导模式（50.65ms，19.74Hz）和较慢语义模式（149.90-164.57ms），通过知识保留微调保持语义能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在操作任务中表现优异，但空中部署面临严格机载计算和通信约束下的低延迟闭环引导挑战。

Method: 1) 紧凑型256M参数VLA系统；2) 双速率调度器，优先处理反应性动作令牌；3) 知识保留微调策略，混合反应性飞行数据、空中语义数据和通用字幕/VQA监督。

Result: 动作分支达到50.65ms（19.74Hz）的边缘推理速率，语义输出在149.90-164.57ms（6.08-6.67Hz），动作分支速率优于AnywhereVLA、FutureVLA和ReMem-VLA等最新架构。

Conclusion: 在紧凑边缘场景下，端到端延迟主要由多模态预填充主导，而非解码少量额外令牌的边际成本；知识保留微调可在不丧失描述能力的前提下实现专业化。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown strong semantic grounding and task generalization in manipulation, but aerial deployment remains difficult because drones require low-latency closed-loop guidance under strict onboard compute and communication constraints. We present LiteVLA-H, a compact 256M-parameter VLA system designed for dual-rate operation on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin: a fast outer-loop guidance mode for short action-token outputs and a slower semantic mode for scene understanding, hazard description, and operator-facing narration. The central empirical observation is that, in this compact edge regime, end-to-end latency is dominated by multimodal pre-fill rather than by the marginal cost of decoding a few extra tokens. This motivates a scheduler that issues reactive action tokens at 50.65,ms (19.74,Hz) while still supporting sentence-level semantic outputs at 149.90--164.57\ms (6.08--6.67,Hz) on the same embedded platform. To specialize the model without collapsing its descriptive competence, we use a knowledge-preserving fine-tuning recipe that mixes reactive flight data, aerial semantic data, and generic caption/VQA supervision. Beyond reporting current latency measurements, we position the system against recent state-of-the-art architectures, including AnywhereVLA, FutureVLA, and ReMem-VLA, showing that the measured action branch reaches a higher edge inference rate under our deployment conditions while retaining periodic semantic awareness.

</details>


### [7] [Selective Correlation Based Knowledge Distillation for Ground Reaction Force Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00888)
*Eun Som Jeon,Jisoo Lee,Huisu Lim,Omik M. Save,Hyunglae Lee,Pavan Turaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于选择性相关知识蒸馏（SCKD）的方法，从可穿戴鞋垫传感器数据中估计地面反作用力（GRF），在保持高精度的同时降低计算资源需求，实现便携式步态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GRF测量依赖实验室测力台，成本高且不便携；可穿戴鞋垫传感器虽便携但易受噪声干扰，精度低；现有深度学习方法计算资源需求大，难以在便携设备上实时分析。

Method: 提出SCKD框架，利用选择性特征考虑时序特性提取相关图进行知识迁移，通过教师-学生架构蒸馏出紧凑模型，增强可解释性并缓解高维数据处理问题。

Result: 在不同步行速度和窗口大小下，SCKD生成的紧凑模型在GRF估计上优于现有方法，验证了其有效性和资源效率。

Conclusion: SCKD为可穿戴步态分析提供了可靠且资源高效的解决方案，兼顾精度与便携性。

Abstract: Wearable sensor-based human gait analysis holds great promise in healthcare, rehabilitation, clinical diagnosis and monitoring, and sports activities. Specifically, ground reaction force (GRF) provides essential insights into the body's interaction with the ground during movement and is typically measured using instrumented treadmills equipped with force plates. However, such equipment is expensive and restricted to laboratory environments. To enable a more portable solution, wearable insole sensors have been used to measure GRF. These sensors, however, are prone to noise and external interference, which reduces measurement accuracy. Deep learning methodologies could be adopted to address these issues, but they often require significant computing resources to achieve high accuracy, limiting their applicability for real-time analysis on portable devices. To overcome these limitations, we propose Selective Correlation Based Knowledge Distillation (SCKD) for estimating GRF from data collected by insole sensors. Our proposed method utilizes selected features considering temporal characteristics in the process of extracting correlation maps for knowledge transfer, enhancing interpretability and mitigating issues in high dimensional data processing. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the compact models generated by our distillation framework through comparison with existing methods. Various configurations of teacher-student architectures and training approaches are examined based on multiple evaluation criteria, utilizing data collected at different walking speeds and with different window sizes. Experimental results confirm that our approach outperforms existing methods in estimating GRF from wearable insole sensor data. Therefore, our approach offers a reliable and resource-efficient solution for human gait analysis.

</details>


### [8] [Skeleton-Based Posture Classification to Promote Safer Walker-Assisted Gait in Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00890)
*Sergio D. Sierra M.,Monica Sinha,Marcela Múnera,Carlos A. Cifuentes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了几种机器学习模型（几何方法、XGBoost、SVM及多种深度学习架构）在智能助行器中对用户行为（助行器选择、站/坐状态、姿态）进行分类的效果。XGBoost在二分类任务中表现近乎完美（助行器选择99.84%，站/坐99.69%），几何方法在8种姿态分类中达89.9%，XGBoost在17种姿态分类训练中达99.24%。深度学习模型（4层CNN、编码器-解码器CNN）在二分类中准确率超98%。研究表明机器学习有望增强智能助行器的人机交互，助力防跌倒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒是严重的公共卫生问题，导致重伤、失去独立性和医疗成本增加。现有智能助行器需要更精准的行为识别能力以提升人机交互和防跌倒效果，但缺乏对多种机器学习模型在助行器使用场景中分类性能的系统评估。

Method: 对比评估几何方法、XGBoost、SVM以及多种深度学习架构（包括4层CNN、编码器-解码器CNN等）在三个分类任务上的性能：助行器选择（二分类）、站/坐状态（二分类）和姿态分类（多分类）。

Result: XGBoost在二分类任务中表现最佳：助行器选择99.84%、站/坐99.69%。几何方法在8姿态分类中达89.9%，XGBoost在17姿态分类训练中达99.24%。4层CNN和编码器-解码器CNN在二分类中准确率超98%。

Conclusion: 机器学习（尤其是XGBoost和几何方法）在智能助行器行为分类中展现出高精度潜力，可有效增强人机交互，为老年人防跌倒提供技术支持。

Abstract: Falls among older adults are a significant public health concern, leading to severe injuries, loss of independence, and increased healthcare costs. This study evaluates the effectiveness of various models, including a Geometric approach, XGBoost, SVM, and several deep learning architectures, in classifying walker usage, standing vs. sitting, and posture for smart walkers used. Geometric and XGBoost were the top performers. XGBoost achieved near-perfect training accuracy in binary classification tasks, with 99.84% for walker choice and 99.69% for standing vs. sitting. For posture classification, Geometric approach attained 89.9% accuracy for 8 postures, and XGBoost obtained 99.24% during training for 17 postures. Deep learning models such as the 4-layer CNN and Encoder-Decoder CNN also demonstrated strong performance in binary classification, with accuracies above 98%. This study underscores the potential of machine learning to enhance human-robot interaction in smart walkers, particularly for fall prevention.

</details>


### [9] [X2SAM: Any Segmentation in Images and Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00891)
*Hao Wang,Limeng Qiao,Chi Zhang,Lin Ma,Guanglu Wan,Xiangyuan Lan,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X2SAM是一个统一的分割多模态大语言模型，通过耦合LLM与Mask Memory模块，将任意分割能力从图像扩展到视频，支持多种分割任务（通用、开放词汇、指代、推理、对话生成、交互式、视觉接地），并引入视频视觉接地（V-VGD）基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs具备图像级理解但像素级感知有限，SAM系列依赖低级视觉提示无法理解复杂指令，已有的分割MLLMs通常专攻图像或视频且不支持文本与视觉提示的统一接口。

Method: 将LLM与Mask Memory模块耦合，存储引导视觉特征以生成时序一致的视频掩码；采用统一的联合训练策略在异构图像和视频数据集上训练。

Result: 在视频分割任务上表现优异，在图像分割基准上保持竞争力，同时保留图像和视频的通用对话能力。

Conclusion: X2SAM实现了从图像到视频的统一任意分割，支持多模态提示输入，在多种分割任务上均有效。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong image-level visual understanding and reasoning, yet their pixel-level perception across both images and videos remains limited. Foundation segmentation models such as the SAM series produce high-quality masks, but they rely on low-level visual prompts and cannot natively interpret complex conversational instructions. Existing segmentation MLLMs narrow this gap, but are usually specialized for either images or videos and rarely support both textual and visual prompts in one interface. We introduce X2SAM, a unified segmentation MLLM that extends any-segmentation capabilities from images to videos. Given conversational instructions and visual prompts, X2SAM couples an LLM with a Mask Memory module that stores guided vision features for temporally consistent video mask generation. The same formulation supports generic, open-vocabulary, referring, reasoning, grounded conversation generation, interactive, and visual grounded segmentation across image and video inputs. We further introduce the Video Visual Grounded (V-VGD) segmentation benchmark, which evaluates whether a model can segment object tracks in videos from interactive visual prompts. With a unified joint training strategy over heterogeneous image and video datasets, X2SAM delivers strong video segmentation performance, remains competitive on image segmentation benchmarks, and preserves general image and video chat ability.

</details>


### [10] [When To Adapt? Adapting the Model or Data in Federated Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00892)
*Chamani Shiranthika,Parvaneh Saeedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了联邦医学影像中两种主流域适应策略——数据层协调（Harmonization）和模型层个性化（Personalization），在六种医学影像任务中的表现，揭示了基于域偏移类型（外观 vs. 结构）的条件性权衡，并提出了策略选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在医学影像中面临客户端域异构性问题，现有方法分为数据层协调和模型层个性化两大范式，但缺乏系统的比较研究，无法为实际应用提供策略选择依据。

Method: 在六种医学影像场景（结肠息肉、皮肤病变、乳腺肿瘤分割；结核CXR、脑肿瘤、乳腺肿瘤分类）中，统一框架下评估多种SOTA协调与个性化方法，覆盖不同类型和程度的域偏移。

Result: 协调方法在外观主导的域变异（如CXR分类）中更有效；个性化方法在结构差异主导的场景（如结肠息肉分割）中表现更优；当客户端间变异较小时，两种策略性能相近。

Conclusion: 联邦医学影像中适应策略的有效性取决于域偏移的类型和程度，而非单一策略本身，研究为策略选择提供了实用指南，并指出了未来混合方法的研发方向。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training across medical institutions without sharing raw data, but its performance is often limited by domain heterogeneity across clients. Existing approaches to address this challenge fall into two main paradigms: model-side personalization, which adapts model parameters to each client, and data-side harmonization, which reduces inter-client variation at the input level. Despite their widespread use, these strategies have not been systematically compared. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive study across six medical imaging settings-colon polyp, skin lesion, and breast tumor segmentation, and tuberculosis CXR, brain tumor, and breast tumor classification-covering diverse types of domain shift. We evaluate a broad set of state-of-the-art harmonization and personalization methods under a unified framework. Our results reveal a conditional trade-off driven by the nature of heterogeneity: harmonization is more effective when variation is primarily appearance-based (e.g., CXR classification), while personalization performs better when differences are structural (e.g., colon polyp segmentation). When inter-client variation is limited, both strategies perform similarly. These findings demonstrate that the effectiveness of adaptation in federated medical imaging depends on the type and magnitude of domain shift rather than the strategy alone. We provide practical guidelines for selecting between harmonization and personalization and highlight directions for future hybrid approaches that combine both paradigms. Code is available at https://github.com/ChamaniS/WhenToAdapt.

</details>


### [11] [Retrieval-Guided Generation for Safer Histopathology Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00893)
*Md. Enamul Hoq,Wataru Uegami,Saghir Alfasly,Ghazal Alabtah,Sahar Rahimi Malakshan,Armita Kazemi,Alex T. Schmitgen,Fred Prior,H. R. Tizhoosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了检索引导生成（RGG）作为全生成式模型在病理学图像描述中的更安全替代方案。在ARCH组织病理学数据集上，RGG相比MedGemma模型显著提升了语义对齐（余弦相似度≈0.60 vs ≈0.47），且置信区间无重叠，表明增益稳健。病理学家定性评审显示RGG更好地保留了形态学术语，减少了无依据诊断，但也揭示了概念混合和继承性过度标签等失败模式。总体而言，检索引导式描述比全生成式方法更透明、可靠，便于审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视觉语言模型在医学图像描述中容易出现幻觉、过度诊断声明和事实不一致等严重问题，这在病理学领域尤为危险。因此需要探索更安全、更可靠的替代方案。

Method: 采用检索引导生成（RGG）方法，即从视觉相似病例的专家文本中总结生成描述，而非从头生成。在ARCH组织病理学数据集上进行实验，并与MedGemma模型对比。

Result: RGG在语义对齐上显著优于MedGemma（余弦相似度≈0.60 vs ≈0.47），置信区间无重叠。病理学家定性评审显示RGG更好地保留了形态学术语，减少了无依据诊断，但也存在概念混合和继承性过度标签等失败模式。

Conclusion: 检索引导式描述比全生成式方法更透明、可靠，提供了更清晰的审计机会，是病理学图像描述中更有前景的安全替代方案。

Abstract: Generative vision-language models can produce fluent medical image captions but remain prone to hallucination, over-specific diagnostic claims, and factual inconsistency-serious issues in pathology. We investigate retrieval-guided generation (RGG) as a safer alternative, where captions are formed by summarizing expert text from visually similar cases rather than generated de novo. On the ARCH histopathology dataset, RGG improves semantic alignment with ground truth, achieving cosine similarity of $\approx$0.60 versus $\approx$0.47 from MedGemma, with non-overlapping confidence intervals indicating a robust gain. A pathologist-led qualitative review shows better preservation of morphology-relevant terminology and fewer unsupported diagnoses, while revealing failure modes such as concept mixing and inherited over-specific labeling. Overall, retrieval-guided captioning offers a more transparent and reliable approach with clearer opportunities for auditing than fully generative methods.

</details>


### [12] [When Less Is More: Simplicity Beats Complexity for Physics-Constrained InSAR Phase Unwrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00896)
*Prabhjot Singh,Manmeet Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模消融实验证明，在InSAR相位解缠任务中，简单的U-Net（7.76M参数）在精度（R²=0.834，RMSE=1.01cm）和推理速度（2.92ms）上全面优于复杂的注意力机制模型（11.37M参数），后者引入的伪高频伪影违反了地球物理形变的平滑性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前InSAR火山与地震监测中，操作相位解缠是主要计算瓶颈，而业界盲目追求高复杂度计算机视觉架构（如注意力机制）用于受物理约束的地球物理回归任务，缺乏对其适用性的验证。

Method: 在包含20个帧、39,724个图块、6.51亿像素的全球LiCSAR基准上，进行了首个大规模架构消融实验，对比了vanilla U-Net与注意力机制模型，并通过功率谱密度（PSD）分析从物理角度解释性能差异。

Result: Vanilla U-Net（7.76M参数）以R²=0.834和RMSE=1.01cm超越注意力机制模型（11.37M参数）34%的R²和51%的RMSE；推理延迟2.92ms，比对手快2.5倍；注意力模型在>0.3 cycles/pixel频段引入非物理高频伪影。

Conclusion: 卷积局部性优于现代复杂架构（注意力机制）用于平滑场回归任务，倡导ML4RS中应遵循物理约束的简洁性设计，以弥合"发表到实践"的鸿沟。

Abstract: Operational phase unwrapping is the primary computational bottleneck in InSAR-based volcanic and seismic monitoring. We challenge the industry trend of adopting high-complexity computer vision architectures, such as attention mechanisms, without validating their suitability for physics-constrained geophysical regression. We present the first large-scale architectural ablation study on a global LiCSAR benchmark (20 frames, 39,724 patches, 651M pixels). Our results reveal a significant "complexity penalty": a vanilla U-Net (7.76M parameters) achieves $R^2=0.834$ and RMSE $= 1.01$ cm, outperforming 11.37M-parameter attention-based models by 34% in $R^2$ and 51% in RMSE. Power Spectral Density (PSD) analysis provides the physical justification: while attention excels at capturing sharp semantic edges in natural images, it injects unphysical high-frequency artifacts ($>0.3$ cycles/pixel) into geophysical fields, violating the fundamental smoothness constraints of elastic surface deformation. With a 2.92ms inference latency (a $2.5\times$ speedup), the vanilla U-Net is the only candidate to comfortably meet the sub-100ms requirement for operational early-warning systems. This work bridges the "publication-to-practice" gap by proving that convolutional locality outperforms modern complexity for smooth-field regression, advocating for physics-informed simplicity in ML4RS. Code available at https://github.com/prabhjotschugh/When-Less-is-More-InSAR-Phase-Unwrapping

</details>


### [13] [LatentDiff: Scaling Semantic Dataset Comparison to Millions of Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00899)
*James Flora,Kowshik Thopalli,Akshay R. Kulkarni,Weng-Keen Wong,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LatentDiff框架，在预训练视觉编码器的潜在空间中通过稀疏自编码器散度检验和密度比估计进行语义数据集比较，并引入Noisy-Diff基准测试，在低至<1%图像存在语义差异的极端设置下仍保持高精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于字幕的语义数据集比较方法计算成本高昂，且对稀疏分布偏移不敏感，亟需一种高效、可扩展且能识别可解释语义差异的方法。

Method: 在预训练视觉编码器的潜在空间中，结合稀疏自编码器（SAE）的散度检验与密度比估计，实现语义数据集间的可解释比较。

Result: 在包含从5%到<1%不同比例语义差异图像的极端稀疏设置下，LatentDiff相比基于字幕的基线方法具有更优的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LatentDiff能够以极低计算成本高效识别数据集间的可解释语义差异，且对极稀疏的分布偏移具有强鲁棒性，为大规模语义数据比较提供了可行的新方案。

Abstract: We present LatentDiff, a scalable framework for semantic dataset comparison that operates directly in the latent space of pretrained vision encoders. By combining sparse autoencoder-based divergence testing with density ratio estimation, LatentDiff identifies interpretable semantic differences between datasets at a fraction of the computational cost of caption-based alternatives. We also introduce Noisy-Diff, a benchmark capturing realistic sparse distribution shifts that cause existing methods to struggle. Experiments demonstrate that LatentDiff achieves superior accuracy while remaining robust to settings where an extremely small fraction of images (from 5% to <1% ) differ semantically.

</details>


### [14] [Validation of Whole-Slide Foundation Models for Image Retrieval in TCGA Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00902)
*Tianhao Lei,Parsa Esmaeilkhani,Saghir Alfasly,Wataru Uegami,Judy C. Boughey,Matthew P. Goetz,Krishna R. Kalari,H. R. Tizhoosh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在9,387张TCGA全切片图像上系统比较了十种检索管线，发现：不同器官和诊断类型间的性能差异大于不同架构间的差异；TITAN模型表现最佳但优势微弱，ABMIL和基于补丁的方法可达到相当水平；性能主要驱动力是补丁级特征表示，而非切片级聚合。即使最佳模型在TCGA上的检索准确率也仅约68%±21%，部分亚型在所有方法上准确率为0%，揭示了基于形态学表征的根本局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确基础模型在全切片图像检索中相对于强基线和监督聚合方法的实际价值，评估不同架构在不同器官和诊断条件下的性能差异。

Method: 在TCGA数据集（9,387张诊断切片，17个器官，60种诊断）上采用留一患者法评估十种检索管线，包括四种预训练的切片基础模型、基于注意力机制的多实例学习（ABMIL）聚合器以及五种采样密度下的补丁级检索。

Result: 器官和诊断类型间的性能差异大于架构间差异；TITAN模型总体最优但优势微弱；补丁级特征表示是性能的主要驱动力，切片级聚合收益有限；最佳模型准确率仅约68%±21%，部分亚型在所有方法上准确率为0%。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍最优的架构，应支持器官级基准测试、诊断感知或集成策略、更强的特征表示以及多模态检索框架；当前的形态学表征存在根本局限，距离可靠的临床部署仍需实质性进展。

Abstract: Foundation models are reshaping computational histopathology, yet their value for whole-slide image retrieval relative to strong patch-based and supervised aggregation baselines remains unclear. We benchmarked ten pipelines on 9,387 diagnostic slides spanning 17 organs and 60 diagnoses from The Cancer Genome Atlas (TCGA) using patient-level leave-one-patient-out evaluation. Methods included four pre-trained slide foundation models, a supervised attention-based multiple instance learning (ABMIL) aggregator on patch embeddings, and patch-level retrieval across five sampling densities.
  Performance varied more across organs and diagnoses than across architectures. Although the slide foundation model TITAN achieved the strongest overall results, its advantage was modest; ABMIL and patch-based methods reached comparable Top-1 and Top-3 accuracy, with no model consistently dominant. Morphologically distinctive entities approached ceiling performance, while rare, heterogeneous, and closely related subtypes remained challenging. Misclassifications aligned with organs exhibiting known inter-observer variability, suggesting an intrinsic ceiling for morphology-only retrieval.
  Performance was driven primarily by patch-level feature representations, with limited benefit from slide-level aggregation, indicating aggregation may be unnecessary in many settings. These findings argue against a universally optimal architecture and instead support organ-resolved benchmarking, diagnosis-aware or ensemble strategies, stronger feature representations, and multimodal retrieval frameworks. Notably, even the best model achieved only $\approx 68\% \pm 21\%$ retrieval accuracy on TCGA, and some subtypes showed $0\%$ accuracy across all methods, highlighting fundamental limitations of morphology-based representations and the need for substantial progress before reliable clinical deployment.

</details>


### [15] [Generalized Category Discovery under Domain Shifts: From Vision to Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00906)
*Hongjun Wang,Po Hu,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对广义类别发现（GCD）在域偏移场景下的问题，提出了三种适配不同基础模型（自监督视觉模型和视觉-语言模型）的框架：HiLo通过多级特征提取和互信息最小化解耦域与语义特征；HLPrompt引入语义感知空间提示微调抑制背景和域噪声；VLPrompt利用视觉-语言模型通过分解文本提示和跨模态一致性正则化。三种方法共享核心设计原则，在合成 corruptions 和真实多域偏移数据集上均显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD方法假设数据来自单一域，但现实中的未标注数据常同时存在域偏移和语义偏移，亟需研究域偏移下的GCD问题。

Method: 提出了三种框架：(i) HiLo：多级特征提取+互信息最小化解耦域和语义特征，结合PatchMix增强和课程采样；(ii) HLPrompt：在HiLo基础上引入语义感知空间提示微调抑制背景和域噪声；(iii) VLPrompt：利用视觉-语言模型，通过分解文本提示和跨模态一致性正则化。

Result: 在合成 corruptions 和真实多域偏移数据集上的大量实验表明，三种方法均显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 三种方法共享核心设计原则但适配不同基础模型骨架，适合不同的部署场景，有效解决了域偏移下的广义类别发现问题。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) aims to categorize unlabelled instances from both known and unknown classes by transferring knowledge from labelled data of known classes. Existing methods assume all data comes from a single domain, yet real-world unlabelled data often exhibits domain shifts alongside semantic shifts. We study GCD under domain shifts and propose three frameworks that adapt foundation models, ranging from self-supervised vision models to vision-language models. (i) HiLo disentangles domain and semantic features through multi-level feature extraction and mutual information minimization, combined with PatchMix augmentation and curriculum sampling. (ii) HLPrompt extends HiLo with semantic-aware spatial prompt tuning to suppress background and domain noise. (iii) VLPrompt leverages vision-language models via factorized textual prompts and cross-modal consistency regularization. The three methods share core design principles while operating on different foundation backbones, making them suitable for different deployment scenarios. Extensive experiments on synthetic corruptions and real-world multi-domain shifts demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/hilo/

</details>


### [16] [TRIP-Evaluate: An Open Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Large Models in Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00907)
*Han Gong,Zhen Zhou,Yunyang Shi,Yan Tan,Jinbiao Huo,Qi Hong,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRIP-Evaluate是一个面向交通运输领域的开放多模态大模型基准，包含837个项目（文本596、图像198、点云43），采用角色-任务-知识分类体系，支持从整体精度到特定故障模式的细粒度诊断。评估发现模型在文本任务上表现提升，但在多步工程计算、规则约束推理、多模态场景理解和点云理解方面仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用基准无法验证大模型在交通运输领域（规则密集型、计算密集型、安全关键型、多模态）的正确性，而少量公开的交通基准范围狭窄且缺乏对文本、图像、点云数据的细粒度诊断能力。

Method: 构建了包含837个项目的多模态基准，采用角色-任务-知识分类体系（覆盖车辆、交通管理、出行者、规划与设计四大职能），每个项目标注了能力、模态和难度标签，并标准化了项目构建、质量控制、提示、解码和评分流程。

Result: 多模型评估显示：文本任务性能在提升，但多步工程计算、规则约束推理、多模态场景理解和点云理解仍存在显著弱点。

Conclusion: TRIP-Evaluate为交通运输领域的模型选择、回归测试和安全部署提供了可复现、可诊断且与工程对齐的评估基线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and multimodal large models (MLLMs) are increasingly used for transportation tasks such as regulation question answering, traffic management support, engineering review, and autonomous-driving scene reasoning. Yet transportation workflows are rule-intensive, computation-intensive, safety-critical, and inherently multimodal. Existing general benchmarks provide limited evidence of whether a model can apply regulations correctly, perform verifiable engineering calculations, or interpret traffic scenes reliably, while the small number of public transportation benchmarks remain narrow in scope and rarely support fine-grained diagnosis across text, images, and point-cloud data. To address this gap, we present TRIP-Evaluate, an open multimodal benchmark for large models in transportation. The benchmark organizes 837 items using a role-task-knowledge taxonomy that covers vehicle, traffic-management, traveler, and planning-and-design functions. Each item is annotated with capability, modality, and difficulty labels, enabling diagnosis from overall accuracy down to specific failure modes. The current release includes 596 text items, 198 image items, and 43 point-cloud items. TRIP-Evaluate also standardizes item construction, quality control, prompting, decoding, and scoring to improve cross-model comparability. Results on a diverse panel of models show that text-based performance is improving, but substantial weaknesses remain in multi-step engineering calculation, rule-constrained reasoning, multimodal scene understanding, and point-cloud understanding. Overall, TRIP-Evaluate provides a reproducible, diagnosable, and engineering-aligned evaluation baseline for model selection, regression testing, and safer deployment in transportation applications.

</details>


### [17] [When Good OCR Is Not Enough: Benchmarking OCR Robustness for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00911)
*Lin Sun,Wang Dexian,Jingang Huang,Linglin Zhang,Change Jia,Zhengwei Cheng,Xiangzheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InduOCRBench，一个面向工业RAG系统的OCR基准测试，涵盖11类复杂文档。评估表明传统字符级指标无法反映下游RAG性能，高OCR精度不保证强RAG效果，结构性和语义错误即使在低WER/CER下也会导致检索失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR基准依赖字符级指标（如WER/CER），无法衡量真实工业场景中OCR对下游RAG系统的影响，需要更贴近实际应用的评估方法。

Method: 构建涵盖极端布局、高分辨率页面、复杂/水印背景、非标准阅读顺序的历史文档、装饰文字、表格和数学公式等11类挑战性文档的基准，在OCR-first RAG流水线中评估最新SOTA OCR模型。

Result: SOTA模型在工业文档上性能明显下降；高OCR精度不保证强RAG性能；结构性和语义错误即使字符级指标较低也会导致检索失败；这种不匹配具有类别依赖性，同时在检索端和生成端均有体现，且在多种OCR-first流水线选择下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 现有OCR评估指标不足以预测工业RAG系统的实际表现，需要面向下游任务的结构化和语义级评估方法。

Abstract: Industrial Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems depend on optical character recognition (OCR) to transform visual documents into text. Existing OCR benchmarks rely on character-level metrics, which inadequately measure downstream RAG effectiveness under real-world conditions. We introduce an OCR benchmark for industrial RAG systems covering 11 challenging document types, including extreme layouts, high-resolution pages, complex or watermarked backgrounds, historical documents with non-standard reading orders, visually decorated text, and documents containing tables and mathematical formulas. Evaluating recent SOTA OCR models under a controlled OCR-first RAG pipeline shows clear performance degradation on realistic industrial documents despite strong conventional benchmark scores. We find that high OCR accuracy does not necessarily translate into strong downstream RAG performance: structural and semantic errors can cause substantial retrieval failures even when WER/CER remains low. Further analysis shows that this mismatch is category-dependent, arises through both retrieval-side and downstream generation-side failures, and remains stable across representative OCR-first pipeline choices. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/Qihoo360/InduOCRBench.

</details>


### [18] [Object-Level Explanations for Image Geolocation Models: a GeoGuessr use-case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00912)
*Emilie Durrieu,Christophe Hurter,Philippe Muller,Victor Boutin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向对象的分析流程，用于解析地理定位模型所依赖的视觉证据。通过从归因图中提取显著区域并分割成类目标元素，结合删除/插入测试验证其预测相关性。在三国家基准实验中发现，归因引导裁剪比随机裁剪保留了更多预测信息，表明归因图可分解为可解释的感知元素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理定位模型的归因方法（如Grad-CAM）倾向于突出扩散区域而非连贯的视觉实体，难以将模型预测与具体物体或可感知模式联系起来。因此，需要一种面向对象的分析方法来揭示模型所使用的视觉证据。

Method: 提出面向对象分析流程：(1) 从归因图提取显著区域；(2) 将显著区域分割为类目标元素；(3) 通过删除测试和插入测试评估这些元素的预测相关性；(4) 将归因引导裁剪与随机裁剪进行对比。

Result: 在三国家基准数据集上，归因引导裁剪相比随机裁剪始终保留了更多预测信息，说明归因图可以被分解为可解释的、可感知的元素。

Conclusion: 归因图能够分解为可解释的感知元素，这为地理定位模型的物体级分析迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: When humans play geolocation games such as GeoGuessr, they rely on concrete visual cues, such as road markings, vegetation, or architectural details, to infer where an image was captured. Whether image geolocation models rely on similar object-level evidence remains difficult to determine, as attribution methods like Grad-CAM typically highlight diffuse regions rather than coherent visual entities, making it difficult to link model predictions to specific objects or perceptible patterns. In this work, we propose an object-centric analysis pipeline to investigate the visual evidence used by geolocation models. Starting from attribution maps, we extract salient regions and segment them into object-like elements. We evaluate their predictive relevance through deletion and insertion tests, comparing attributionguided crops to randomly selected regions with similar coverage. Experiments on a three-country benchmark show that attribution-guided crops consistently retain more information for the model's prediction than random crops. These results suggest that attribution maps can be decomposed into interpretable, perceptible elements, providing a step toward object-level analysis of geolocation models.

</details>


### [19] [SAMamba3D: adapting Segment Anything for generalizable 3D segmentation of multiphase pore-scale images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00916)
*Rui Zhang,Xianzhi Song,Linqi Zhu,Branko Bijeljic,Gensheng Li,Martin J. Blunt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAMamba3D是一种参数高效的框架，通过将冻结的SAM编码器与Mamba体积上下文建模和跨尺度特征交互结合，实现了对多相孔隙尺度X射线岩石图像的通用三维分割，在不同岩石类型、流体和扫描条件下均达到或超越现有基线，且减少了对特定数据集重新训练的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前三维孔隙尺度图像分割方法通常针对特定数据集，当岩石类型、流体模式、扫描仪或采集条件变化时需要重新训练或大量微调，缺乏泛化能力。虽然SAM提供强大的二维边界先验，但无法直接应用于三维数据。

Method: 提出SAMamba3D框架，采用参数高效策略：保留大部分冻结的SAM编码器，将其与基于Mamba的体积上下文建模和渐进式跨尺度特征交互相结合，实现从二维到三维的适应。

Result: 在砂岩和碳酸盐岩数据集上，针对不同流体、润湿性和扫描条件，SAMamba3D匹配或超越当前三维基线方法，同时减少了对特定案例重新训练的需求。分割图像保留了流体饱和度、连通性和界面形态等物理有意义的描述符。

Conclusion: SAMamba3D实现了更可靠、更快速的大规模三维多相图像分析，为孔隙尺度分割提供了一种泛化能力更强的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable segmentation of multiphase pore-scale X-ray images of rocks is necessary to quantify fluid saturation, connectivity, and interfacial geometry. However, current 3D segmentation methods are typically dataset-specific, requiring retraining or extensive fine-tuning whenever rock type, fluid pattern, scanner, or acquisition conditions change. Foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) provide strong 2D boundary priors, but they are not directly applicable to 3D data.
  We present SAMamba3D, a parameter-efficient framework that adapts a largely frozen SAM encoder to generalizable 3D pore-scale segmentation by coupling it with Mamba-based volumetric context modeling and progressive cross-scale feature interaction. For sandstone and carbonate datasets, with different fluids, wettability, and scanning conditions, SAMamba3D matches or outperforms current 3D baselines while reducing the need for case-specific retraining. The resulting segmented images preserve physically meaningful descriptors, including fluid saturation, connectivity, and interface morphology, enabling more reliable and rapid analysis of large 3D multiphase images.

</details>


### [20] [WildTableBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Foundation Models on Table Understanding In the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01018)
*Junzhe Huang,Xiaoxiao Sun,Yan Yang,Yuxuan Hou,Ruotian Zhang,Sirui Li,Hehe Fan,Serena Yeung-Levy,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildTableBench是一个针对真实世界表格图像问答的基准测试，包含402张高信息密度表格图像和928个手工标注问题，覆盖17个子类型。评估21个多模态基础模型后，仅有一个模型准确率超过50%，其余模型在4.1%-49.9%之间，揭示了当前模型在结构感知和推理方面的持续弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态表格理解评测大多基于结构化文本或干净渲染图像，忽略了真实世界表格图像在布局多样性、领域广泛性等方面的视觉复杂性，缺乏对模型在真实场景中结构感知和数值推理能力的系统评估。

Method: 构建WildTableBench基准：从在线论坛和网站收集402张真实世界高信息密度表格图像，手工标注928个问答对（17个子类型、5大类别），并基于该基准评估21个前沿商业和开源多模态基础模型。

Result: 仅一个模型准确率超过50%（约50%+），其余模型准确率在4.1%至49.9%之间，整体表现不佳。诊断分析表明模型在结构感知和推理方面存在持续性弱点。

Conclusion: WildTableBench可作为有价值的诊断基准，用于评测和指导多模态模型在真实世界表格图像理解能力上的提升。

Abstract: Using multimodal foundation models to analyze table images is a high-value yet challenging application in consumer and enterprise scenarios. Despite its importance, current evaluations rely largely on structured-text tables or clean rendered images, leaving the visual complexity of in-the-wild table images underexplored. Such images feature varied layouts and diverse domains that demand sophisticated structural perception and numerical reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce WildTableBench, the first question-answering benchmark for naturally occurring table images from real-world settings. WildTableBench comprises 402 high-information-density table images collected from online forums and websites across diverse domains, together with 928 manually annotated and verified questions spanning 17 subtypes across five categories. We evaluate 21 frontier proprietary and open-source multimodal foundation models on this benchmark. Only one model exceeds 50% accuracy, while all remaining models range from 4.1% to 49.9%. We further conduct diagnostic analyses to characterize model failures and reveal persistent weaknesses in structural perception and reasoning. These results and analyses provide useful insights into current model capabilities and establish WildTableBench as a valuable diagnostic benchmark for table image understanding.

</details>


### [21] [InterPhys: Physics-aware Human Motion Synthesis in a Dynamic Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01036)
*Chaoyue Xing,Wei Mao,Miaomiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理感知的人体运动生成框架，通过显式建模人体-物体、人体-场景及内部身体动力学等全谱系力，并采用连续距离力模型泛化接触建模至任意表面，在动态场景中生成物理一致的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作因接触建模受限（通常仅限手部）而生成物理不真实的人体运动，缺乏对完整力的建模。

Method: 提出物理感知的运动生成框架，显式建模人体相关全谱系力；引入软物理约束维持力与力矩平衡；提出连续距离力模型，将接触泛化至任意表面，支持静态环境与动态物体交互。

Result: 显著提升物理合理性，在复杂场景中具有良好泛化能力，为物理一致的人体运动生成建立了新基准。

Conclusion: 通过全谱系力建模与连续距离力模型，有效解决了动态场景下物理感知人体运动合成的物理真实性问题。

Abstract: This paper tackles the problem of physics-aware human motion synthesis in a dynamic scene. Unlike existing works which mainly tend to generate physically unrealistic motions due to limited contact modeling, typically restricted to hands, in this paper, we introduce a physics-aware human motion generation framework that explicitly models the full spectrum of human-related forces, including human-object, human-scene, and internal body dynamics.~Our method imposes soft physical constraints to maintain force and torque balance, ensuring physically grounded motion synthesis. We further propose a novel continuous distance-based force model that generalizes contact modeling to arbitrary surfaces, capturing interactions not only with static environments but also with dynamic, moving objects. Extensive experiments show that our approach significantly improves physical plausibility and generalizes well to complex scenes, setting a new benchmark for physically consistent human motion generation.

</details>


### [22] [WILD SAM: A Simulated-and-Real Data Augmentation for Autonomous Driving Perception under Challenging Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01081)
*Hamed Khatounabadi,Xiaohu Lu,Hayder Radha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WILD和WILD SAM两种新方法，通过伪标签去噪和混合训练策略，显著提升恶劣天气下目标检测器的性能，在Four Seasons数据集上AP提升达13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测器在恶劣天气下性能大幅下降，造成自动驾驶安全关键性的域偏移问题。现有方法依赖合成数据训练，限制了现实世界的适用性；而伪标签方法因恶劣天气下生成的标签噪声大而未被充分利用。

Method: 提出两种方法：1) WILD框架，用于过滤恶劣天气下真实数据生成的噪声伪标签；2) WILD SAM混合训练方法，同时利用伪标签去噪和基于仿真的训练方案，结合目标域真实恶劣天气数据。

Result: 在Four Seasons数据集的雨雪场景下验证，AP提升最高达13%，显著缩小了与基线之间的天气诱导性能差距。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效缓解了天气引起的域偏移问题，提升了恶劣天气下目标检测的鲁棒性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The performance of state-of-the-art object detectors degrades significantly under adverse weather, causing a safety-critical domain shift problem for autonomous vehicles. Recent efforts address this problem by relying on synthetic data to train the object detectors, which limits their real-world applicability. Meanwhile, pseudo-labeling is widely used for cross-dataset domain adaptation problems. However, these methods have not been exploited by weather-based domain adaptation approaches due to the noisy nature of such labels generated under harsh weather conditions. In this paper, we propose two new approaches to mitigate this weather-induced domain shift. First, we propose a Weather-Induced pseudo Label Denoising (WILD) framework that filters noisy pseudo labels generated by real data captured under adverse weather conditions. Second, we develop a novel hybrid training methodology, WILD SAM, that exploits both pseudo-label denoising and simulation-based training solutions while using real-data from the target harsh-weather domain. We validate both proposed approaches, WILD and WILD SAM, on the recently released Four Seasons dataset across rainy and snowy scenarios. Experiments show that the proposed frameworks improve Average Precision (AP) up to 13\% and significantly reduce the weather-induced performance gap relative to the baseline. The code is available at: https://github.com/Kh-Hamed/WILD-SAM

</details>


### [23] [Patient-Specific Optimization for Mandibular Reconstruction Planning with Enhanced Bone Union](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01084)
*Hamidreza Aftabi,John E. Lloyd,Amanda Ding,Benedikt Sagl,Eitan Prisman,Antony Hodgson,Sidney Fels*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OsteoOpt++，一种面向患者个性化下颌骨重建的图像到决策规划循环系统。该系统通过贝叶斯优化搜索六个临床可控变量，以最大化供体-下颌骨骨接触面积，从而促进骨愈合。在通用缺陷和患者特异性病例中，骨接触面积分别提升最高29个百分点（329%）和26个百分点，且敏感性分析表明模型对参数变化稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管化骨移植下颌骨重建中供体-宿主不愈合问题严重，而现有虚拟手术规划仅生成几何方案，未能明确促进骨愈合的构型。因此需要一种能够显式优化骨接触条件、提高骨愈合率的手术规划方法。

Method: 方法包括四个步骤：(1) 术前CT生成个性化数字孪生（模板-患者配准+CT衍生肌肉和颞下颌关节参数更新）；(2) 贝叶斯优化结合期望改进+采集规则，搜索六个临床可控变量（切面方向和供体放置位置）；(3) 采用骨接触驱动目标函数及其安全因子正则化变体；(4) 在3个通用缺损（体部、联合部、升支-体部）和3+1个患者特异性病例上评估，其中3例用于优化、1例用于验证。

Result: 通用病例中，周期平均供体-下颌骨接触面积提升最高29个百分点（相对提升329%）；患者特异性病例中，相比术后第5天构型提升最高26个百分点。11个建模参数的10%敏感性分析显示目标函数变化在通用病例≤3%、患者病例≤4%。纵向病例中预测接触与1年骨形成的Dice重叠系数为0.70和0.76。

Conclusion: OsteoOpt++为外科医生提供了术前、图像驱动的切面方向和供体放置推荐，能够改善现有手术方案中的骨愈合条件。代码已开源在GitHub上。

Abstract: Mandibular reconstruction with vascularized bone grafts is complicated by donor-host nonunion, and current virtual surgical planning produces a geometric plan rather than a configuration that explicitly promotes bone union. We present OsteoOpt++, an image-to-decision planning loop for patient-specific mandibular reconstruction. A pre-operative computed tomography (CT) is converted into a personalized digital twin through template-to-patient registration and CT-derived updates of the muscle and temporomandibular-joint parameters. Bayesian optimization with an expected-improvement-plus acquisition rule then searches six clinically controllable cut-plane and donor-positioning variables under an apposition-driven objective and a safety-factor-regularized variant. The workflow was evaluated on three generic defects (body, symphysis, and ramus-body) and a total of 3+1 patient-specific cases, with 3 used for optimization and 1 for validation. In the generic cases, against a common surgical approach, cycle-averaged donor-mandible apposition increased by up to 29 percentage points (329% relative); in the patient-specific cases, against the surgeon-implemented day-5 post-operative configuration, by up to 26 percentage points. A 10% sensitivity analysis over eleven modeling parameters capped the change in the apposition-driven objective at 3% for generic cases and 4% for patient-specific cases, and the longitudinal case showed Dice overlap of 0.70 and 0.76 between predicted apposition and year-1 bone formation. Clinically, this provides surgeons with a pre-operative, image-driven recommendation for cut-plane orientation and donor placement that is predicted to improve union conditions over the configurations currently delivered in the operating room. The optimization and patient-specific modeling code is open source at https://github.com/hamidreza-aftabi/OsteoOpt.

</details>


### [24] [Disciplined Diffusion: Text-to-Image Diffusion Model against NSFW Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01113)
*Chi Zhang,Changjia Zhu,Xiaowen Li,Yao Liu,Zhuo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Disciplined Diffusion (DDiffusion)，一种通过隐式语义检测与局部编辑实现鲁棒文本到图像生成的安全框架，避免传统二元过滤器的对抗攻击与高误报问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I扩散模型的安全过滤器存在两个关键缺陷：一是依赖二元阻断（允许/拒绝）策略，容易受到对抗性攻击通过改写关键词绕过检测；二是高误报率损害良性用户的体验。

Method: DDiffusion包含两个核心组件：1) 语义检索机制，基于概念分布而非脆弱的成对相似性评估提示词；2) 扩散过程中的定位方法，仅选择性地编辑生成图像中的有害区域而非整体阻断。

Result: DDiffusion能有效抑制恶意内容生成，同时保持良性提示下的生成保真度，且不暴露二元允许/拒绝信号，从而抵御现有探测攻击。

Conclusion: DDiffusion通过隐式语义检测与局部图像编辑替代传统二元过滤，为T2I扩散模型提供了更鲁棒且用户体验更优的安全机制。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have the ability to build high-quality pictures from text prompts, but they pose safety concerns because they can generate offensive or disturbing imagery when provided with harmful inputs. Existing safety filters typically rely on text-based classifiers or image-based checkers that completely block the output upon detecting a threat, issuing an explicit allow/block feedback signal to the user. This binary strategy leaves models vulnerable to adversarial attacks that alter keywords to bypass detection, and it causes high false-alarm rates that degrade the experience for benign users. To address such vulnerabilities, we propose Disciplined Diffusion (DDiffusion), a novel robust text-to-image diffusion that counters Not Safe For Work (NSFW) generation by uncovering implicit malicious semantics in prompt embeddings. DDiffusion leverages a semantic retrieval mechanism to evaluate prompts against concept distributions rather than relying on brittle pairwise similarity. Furthermore, it employs a localization method during the diffusion process to selectively edit only the harmful regions of the generated image. By returning locally sanitized images instead of applying uniform blocking, DDiffusion suppresses malicious content while preserving generation fidelity for benign prompts and avoiding the binary allow-deny signal on which existing probing attacks rely.

</details>


### [25] [ScribbleEdit: Synthetic Data for Image Editing with Scribbles and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01135)
*Anya Ji,George Ma,Téa Wright,Yiming Zhang,David Chan,Alane Suhr,Somayeh Sojoudi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScribbleEdit，一个大规模合成数据集，将自然语言指令与自由手绘涂鸦结合，用于精确可控的图像编辑。通过合成管道生成源-目标图像对，并用人类绘制的涂鸦和VLM生成的文本指令配对。实验表明，基于该数据集微调的扩散模型和自回归模型在空间对齐和语义一致性编辑上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑方法中，自然语言指令缺乏空间精确性，自由手绘涂鸦无法表达细粒度视觉属性。现有模型难以联合解释抽象涂鸦与文本，主要因为缺乏专门的训练数据。

Method: 构建ScribbleEdit大规模合成数据集：通过基于修复(inpainting)的合成管道自动生成源-目标图像对，再与人工绘制的涂鸦和VLM生成的文本指令配对。基于该数据集微调扩散模型和自回归统一多模态图像编辑模型。

Result: 现成模型难以处理抽象涂鸦输入，而在ScribbleEdit上微调后，模型在生成空间对齐且语义一致的编辑结果方面显著提升。

Conclusion: ScribbleEdit数据集有效弥合了涂鸦空间信息与文本语义信息之间的鸿沟，为多模态图像编辑提供了关键训练资源。

Abstract: Recent progress in generative models has significantly advanced image editing capabilities, yet precise and intuitive user control remains difficult. Specifically, users often struggle to communicate both exact spatial layouts and specific semantic details simultaneously. While natural language instructions effectively convey high-level semantics like texture and color, they lack spatial specificity. Conversely, freehand scribbles provide rough spatial boundaries but cannot express detailed visual attributes. Consequently, achieving precise control requires combining both modalities. However, existing models struggle to jointly interpret abstract scribbles alongside text due to a lack of specialized training data.
  In this work, we introduce ScribbleEdit, a large-scale synthetic dataset designed to bridge this gap by combining natural language instructions with freehand scribble inputs for more accurate, controllable edits. We construct this dataset through a synthetic pipeline that automatically generates source-target image pairs via inpainting, which are then paired with human-drawn scribbles and VLM-generated text instructions. Using ScribbleEdit, we evaluate and finetune both diffusion-based and autoregressive unified multimodal image editing models. Our experiments reveal that while off-the-shelf models struggle with abstract scribble inputs, finetuning on our synthetic dataset significantly improves their ability to generate spatially aligned and semantically consistent edits.

</details>


### [26] [Semantic Context-aware mOdality fUsion Transformer (SCOUT): A Context-Aware Multimodal Transformer for Concept-Grounded Pathology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01144)
*Suryakant Singh,Saarthak Kapse,Joel Saltz,Prateek Prasanna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOUT是一种基于语义上下文感知的多模态融合Transformer框架，用于病理报告生成。它通过渐进式条件化图像表示，整合局部组织模式、全切片上下文和专家语义描述符，在TCGA-BRCA、MICCAI REG和HistAI数据集上达到了最优的BLEU和METEOR指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理学基础模型虽然能生成流畅报告，但缺乏临床依据，难以准确表示病理学家关注的关键诊断概念及其关系，原因在于难以整合从细粒度细胞模式到切片级组织架构再到高层诊断概念的异质视觉证据，同时保持可解释性和临床连贯性。

Method: 提出SCOUT（语义上下文感知多模态融合Transformer），一种上下文感知的概念基础多模态框架。该方法在统一学习范式中整合局部组织学模式、全切片上下文和专家策划的语义描述符，通过深度感知的上下文调制和自适应多模态融合，在编码过程中动态细化视觉特征，生成临床连贯的报告。

Result: 使用CONCH1.5特征，在TCGA-BRCA、MICCAI REG和HistAI数据集上，SCOUT在所有数据集上取得最佳BLEU-1至BLEU-4和METEOR分数，并在TCGA-BRCA和MICCAI REG上取得最佳ROUGE-L。在TCGA-BRCA上达到BLEU-1/2/3/4分别为0.436/0.303/0.202/0.156，METEOR为0.204；在REG 2025上分别达到0.865/0.834/0.805/0.780和0.568。

Conclusion: 渐进式上下文条件化策略能够有效提升病理报告生成的临床依据性和语义准确性，结果支持该方法在临床可靠病理报告生成中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) present a fundamental challenge for computational pathology due to their extreme resolution, multi-scale heterogeneity, and the requirement for clinically reliable interpretation. Although recent pathology foundation models have enabled fluent report generation, they often lack clinical grounding, failing to accurately represent key diagnostic concepts and relationships observed by pathologists. This limitation arises from the difficulty of integrating heterogeneous visual evidence spanning fine-grained cellular patterns, slide-level tissue architecture, and high-level diagnostic concepts, while maintaining interpretability and clinical coherence. Here we present SCOUT: Semantic Context-aware mOdality fUsion Transformer, a context-aware concept-grounded multimodal framework for pathology report generation that enables progressive conditioning of image representations by global slide information and explicit diagnostic concepts. The method integrates local histological patterns, whole-slide context, and expert-curated semantic descriptors within a unified learning paradigm, allowing visual features to be dynamically refined throughout the encoding process. By combining depth-aware contextual modulation with adaptive multimodal fusion during text generation, the framework produces clinically coherent reports while preserving complementarity across representational scales. Using CONCH1.5 features, we evaluate SCOUT against WSI-Caption, HistGen, and BiGen on TCGA-BRCA, MICCAI REG, and HistAI. SCOUT achieves the best BLEU-1 to BLEU-4 and METEOR scores on all datasets, plus the best ROUGE-L on TCGA-BRCA and MICCAI REG. On TCGA-BRCA, it reaches 0.436/0.303/0.202/0.156 BLEU-1/2/3/4 and 0.204 METEOR; on REG 2025, it achieves 0.865/0.834/0.805/0.780 and 0.568. These results support progressive contextual conditioning for grounded pathology report generation.

</details>


### [27] [CEZSAR: A Contrastive Embedding Method for Zero-Shot Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01165)
*Valter Estevam,Rayson Laroca,Helio Pedrini,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对比学习的零样本动作识别方法（CEZSAR），通过构建联合嵌入空间并设计自动负采样策略，在UCF-101和Kinetics-400数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本动作识别中存在语义鸿沟（文本域与视觉域表示不一致）和域偏移（训练集与测试集分布差异）两大核心问题，现有方法对这两方面处理仍不充分。

Method: 提出CEZSAR模型：1）将视频与自然语言描述编码到联合嵌入空间进行对齐训练；2）设计自动负采样程序以生成视觉-不相关描述的非配对数据，增强训练集多样性。

Result: 在UCF-101和Kinetics-400数据集上，多种划分配置下均取得最先进结果。

Conclusion: 基于对比学习的联合嵌入空间结合自动负采样策略有效缓解了语义鸿沟和域偏移问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel Zero-Shot Action Recognition~(ZSAR) method based on contrastive learning. In ZSAR, we aim to classify examples from classes that were missing during training. Two well-known problems remain in ZSAR: the semantic gap and the domain shift. A semantic gap occurs because label representations come from the textual domain (i.e., language models) and must be associated with visual representations (i.e., CNNs, RNNs, transformer-based). This multimodal nature implies that the semantic properties of the two spaces are not identical. On the other hand, the domain shift arises from differences between the training and test sets and is inherent to ZSAR once the test set is unknown. One of the most promising methods to address both issues is learning joint embedding spaces. Therefore, we propose a new model that encodes videos and sentences in a joint embedding space, trained by aligning videos with their natural-language descriptions. We design an automatic negative sampling procedure to augment the training dataset and generate unpaired data, i.e., visual appearance and unrelated descriptions. Our results are state-of-the-art on the UCF-101 and Kinetics-400 datasets under several split configurations. Our code is available at https://github.com/valterlej/cezsar.

</details>


### [28] [CADFit: Precise Mesh-to-CAD Program Generation with Hybrid Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01171)
*Ghadi Nehme,Eamon Whalen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CADFit是一种基于混合优化的CAD重建框架，通过增量拟合和验证参数化操作，从网格中恢复复杂、可编辑的CAD构建序列，在多个基准上优于现有方法，并支持从图像端到端重建CAD序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD重建方法多局限于难以编辑的格式（如网格或Brep）或简单的草图-拉伸流水线及低复杂度数据集，缺乏从几何输入恢复复杂、可编辑的CAD参数化构建序列的有效方法。

Method: 将重建问题形式化为结构化CAD程序上的IoU驱动优化，通过增量拟合和几何反馈验证参数化操作，支持拉伸、旋转、圆角和倒角等多种操作，并集成多模态流水线实现从图像到CAD序列的端到端重建。

Result: 在多个CAD基准上，CADFit在体积IoU和Chamfer距离上优于现有网格到CAD方法，并显著降低了重建CAD程序的有效性比率，尤其对复杂设计效果突出。

Conclusion: CADFit通过准确重建高复杂度CAD模型，为生成更丰富的数据集和推动基于学习的CAD逆向工程方法提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Despite recent progress, recovering parametric CAD construction sequences from geometric input, such as meshes or point clouds, is a key challenge for design and manufacturing, as existing CAD reconstruction and generation methods are largely restricted to difficult-to-edit formats like meshes or Breps or editable simple sketch-and-extrude pipelines and low-complexity datasets. We introduce CADFit, a hybrid optimization-based CAD reconstruction framework that recovers complex, editable CAD construction sequences from meshes by incrementally fitting and validating parametric operations using geometric feedback. Our approach is distinguished by formulating reconstruction as an IoU-driven optimization over structured CAD programs and supporting a rich set of operations, including extrusions, revolutions, fillets, and chamfers. Experiments on multiple CAD benchmarks show that CADFit outperforms state-of-the-art mesh-to-CAD methods in volumetric Intersection-over-Union and Chamfer Distance, while substantially reducing the Invalid Ratio of reconstructed CAD programs, particularly for complex designs. We further present a multimodal pipeline that enables end-to-end reconstruction of CAD construction sequences from images by combining image-based geometry reconstruction with CADFit. By enabling accurate reconstruction of higher-complexity CAD models, CADFit provides a practical foundation for generating richer datasets and advancing future learning-based approaches to CAD reverse engineering.

</details>


### [29] [Phase-map synthesis from magnitude-only MR images using conditional score-based diffusion models with application in training of accelerated MRI reconstruction models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01185)
*M. Berk Sahin,Dilek Yalcinkaya,Abolfazl Hashemi,Behzad Sharif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于条件分数扩散模型(SBDM)从幅度图像合成相位图，生成k空间数据用于训练深度学习MRI重建模型，解决了临床中缺少原始k空间数据的问题。实验表明该方法在重建保真度上优于平滑相位和GAN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床MRI中原始k空间数据因存储和隐私问题被丢弃，仅保留幅度图像，导致深度学习重建模型训练数据匮乏。现有开源k空间数据集有限，而大量幅度图像数据库未被充分利用，亟需利用幅度图像生成高质量k空间数据的方法。

Method: 采用条件分数扩散模型(SBDM)，以幅度MR图像为条件，合成对应的图像域相位图。将合成相位与幅度图像结合生成k空间数据集，用于训练下游的深度学习加速MRI重建模型。

Result: 与平滑相位方法、GAN合成相位方法相比，SBDM合成数据训练的DL模型在定量指标和定性观察上均表现更优，重建图像中的错误或幻觉特征更少，诊断准确性更高。

Conclusion: 基于SBDM的相位合成方法能从幅度图像生成高保真k空间数据，有效利用大规模幅度图像数据库训练通用性深度学习重建模型，为克服临床k空间数据缺失问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) enabled by the training of deep learning (DL)-based image recon. models requires large and diverse raw k-space datasets. In most clinical MRI applications, due to storage and patient privacy concerns, raw k-space data is discarded and magnitude-only images are the only component saved. Consequently, a large portion of the DL-based MRI recon. literature has either relied on small training datasets or has used one of the few available open-source k-space datasets. At the same time, the growing number of anonymized magnitude-only image registries/databases motivates the development of techniques that can use them as training datasets for generalizable DL-based recon. models. Here we propose to address this challenge by employing a generative approach based on conditional score-based diffusion models (SBDMs): given a magnitude-only MR image, it synthesizes a phase map (in the image domain) that realistically corresponds to the magnitude-only image. We evaluate its generative capabilities in a downstream DL-based recon. task whereby a large k-space dataset is generated by combining the SBDM-synthesized phase-maps and the corresponding magnitude-only images, and this k-space dataset is then used to train a DL model for accelerated MRI recon. We compare the performance of the resulting DL model versus those trained according to (a) a naive approach that uses smooth phase, (b) a k-space training dataset generated using synthesized phase maps derived from a generative adversarial network, and (c) the ground truth k-space data. Our results suggest that the DL model trained from SBDM-synthesized k-space data outperforms the other approaches in terms of quantitative metrics as well as qualitatively observed recon. fidelity, i.e., whether the reconstructed images include erroneous or hallucinated features that could adversely impact diagnostic accuracy.

</details>


### [30] [Asymmetric Invertible Threat: Learning Reversible Privacy Defense for Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01217)
*Jiabei Zhang,Ziyuan Yang,Andrew Beng Jin Teoh,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称可逆人脸保护框架(ARFP)，通过密钥条件流形绑定、对抗性恢复感知训练和授权可逆恢复三个组件，在单个框架中集成了隐私保护、密钥恢复和篡改指示功能，有效抵抗恢复攻击的同时保持授权恢复的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸识别对抗隐私保护方法依赖于输入空间扰动来混淆身份信息，但该方法存在缺陷：当对手学习到恢复或纯化映射时，保护效果会大幅下降。现有防御范式无法控制可逆性，导致反向操纵成为可能。

Method: 提出非对称可逆人脸保护框架(ARFP)，包含三个核心组件：(1) 密钥条件流形绑定：将保护变换与用户提供的密钥绑定；(2) 对抗性恢复感知训练：引入替代恢复对手进行对抗训练，提升对逆向纯化攻击的鲁棒性；(3) 授权可逆恢复：支持使用正确密钥恢复，同时提供基于随机数的篡改指示。

Result: 在所考虑威胁模型下的广泛实验表明，ARFP提升了对抗恢复攻击的抵抗力，同时保持了授权恢复的可用性。实验结果提供了在测试环境下密钥敏感的恢复行为和篡改感知能力的实证证据。

Conclusion: ARFP通过将隐私保护、密钥恢复和篡改指示集成在一个框架中，有效解决了现有对抗隐私保护方法在面对恢复攻击时保护效果退化的问题，为非对称对抗攻击场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Face Recognition systems are widely deployed in real-world applications, but they also raise privacy concerns due to unauthorized collection and misuse of facial data. Existing adversarial privacy protection methods rely on input-space perturbations to obfuscate identity information, yet their protection can degrade when adversaries learn restoration or purification mappings that partially invert the transformation. We study this setting as an asymmetric adversarial attack, in which reverse manipulation becomes feasible because existing defense paradigms do not control reversibility. To address this problem, we propose Asymmetric Reversible Face Protection (ARFP), a restoration-aware extension of personalized face cloaking that integrates privacy protection, keyed recovery, and tamper indication in a single framework. ARFP consists of three components: Key-Conditioned Manifold Binding, which ties the protection transformation to a user-provided key; Adversarial Restoration-Aware Training, which introduces a surrogate restoration adversary during training to improve robustness against evaluated inverse purification attacks; and Authorized Reversible Restoration, which supports recovery with the correct key while providing nonce-based tamper indication. Extensive experiments under the threat models considered in this work show that ARFP improves resistance to the evaluated restoration attacks while preserving authorized recovery utility. These results provide empirical evidence of key-sensitive recovery behavior and tamper awareness in the tested settings.

</details>


### [31] [Visual Implicit Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01220)
*Pengfei Jiang,Jixiang Luo,Luxi Lin,Zhaohong Huang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIAR 是一种基于隐式平衡层的视觉自回归模型，通过逐尺度迭代控制实现计算-质量权衡，在 ImageNet 256×256 上以 38.4% 的参数量达到 FID 2.16，并支持推理时动态调整内存和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR 模型在逐尺度预测中固定计算量且高分辨率下显存膨胀，限制了部署灵活性和效率。作者希望设计一种能动态控制计算开销、同时保持生成质量的视觉自回归框架。

Method: 在浅层预/后处理块之间嵌入隐式平衡层（implicit equilibrium layer），并采用 Jacobian-Free Backpropagation 训练，使训练内存恒定；推理时引入逐尺度迭代次数控制旋钮，实现计算量可调。

Result: ImageNet 256×256 上 FID 2.16、sFID 8.07，参数量仅为 VAR 的 38.4%；峰值显存从 19.24 GB 降至 8.53 GB，吞吐量从 15.16 翻倍至 32.08 images/s；零样本修复和类别条件编辑效果优于 VAR。

Conclusion: 隐式平衡层与逐尺度计算控制结合，可有效提升视觉自回归模型的部署灵活性和效率，在不重训练前提下实现质量-效率帕累托最优，适用于实际视觉生成任务。

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive Modeling (VAR) based on next-scale prediction achieves strong generation quality, but their explicit deep stacks fix the amount of computation per scale and inflate memory at high resolutions. We introduce Visual Implicit Autoregressive Modeling (VIAR), a next-scale autoregressive generator that embeds an implicit equilibrium layer between shallow pre/post blocks. The implicit layer is trained with Jacobian-Free Backpropagation, yielding constant training memory, while inference exposes a per-scale iteration knob that enables compute control. On ImageNet 256x256 benchmark, VIAR attains FID 2.16, and sFID 8.07 with only 38.4% parameters of VAR, matching or surpassing strong AR baselines and remaining competitive with large diffusion models. By controlling the per-scale knob, VIAR can reduce peak memory from 19.24 GB to 8.53 GB and doubles throughput from 15.16 to 32.08 images/s on a single RTX 4090, without retraining. Ablations show that fewer steps are sufficient for fixed-point iterations to converge and that VIAR consistently dominates VAR across quality efficiency operating points. In zero shot in-painting and class-conditional editing, VIAR produces sharper details and smoother boundaries while preserving global structure, validating the benefits of implicit equilibria and per-scale compute control for practical, deployable visual generation.

</details>


### [32] [Degradation-Aware Adaptive Context Gating for Unified Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01236)
*Lei He,Jielei Chu,Fengmao Lv,Weide Liu,Tianrui Li,Jun Cheng,Yuming Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DACG-IR，一种通过显式感知退化特征来动态调节特征表示的图像复原统一模型，在多种退化场景下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一模型处理多种图像退化时存在任务干扰问题，现有方法缺乏对退化特性的显式感知能力。

Method: 构建轻量级多尺度退化感知模块提取粗粒度退化信息并生成逐层提示，引导编码器-解码器中的注意力温度和输出门控；同时提出空间-通道双门控自适应融合机制，抑制噪声从浅层向深层传播。

Result: 在单任务、All-in-One、恶劣天气去除以及复合退化设定下均超越现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 显式感知退化特征并动态调制特征表示是提升统一图像复原模型性能的有效方向。

Abstract: Unified image restoration using a single model often faces task interference due to diverse degradations. To address this, we propose DACG-IR (Degradation-Aware Adaptive Context Gating), which enables explicit perception of degradation characteristics to dynamically modulate feature representations. Our method constructs degradation-aware contextual representations from the input to modulate attention distribution, frequency-domain features, and feature aggregation. Specifically, a lightweight multi-scale degradation-aware module extracts coarse degradation information and generates layer-wise prompts. These prompts guide attention temperature and output gating in encoder and decoder blocks for adaptive feature extraction. Additionally, a spatial-channel dual-gated adaptive fusion mechanism refines encoder features, suppressing noise propagation from shallow to deep layers. This design effectively suppresses degradation-induced noise while preserving informative structures. Experiments show DACG-IR outperforms state-of-the-art methods in single-task, all-in-one, adverse weather removal, and composite degradation settings. Code: https://github.com/HlHomes/DACG-IR-code

</details>


### [33] [Exploring Prompt Alignment with Clinical Factors in Zero-Shot Segmentation VLMs for NSCLC Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01266)
*Suraj Pai,Thibault Heintz,Cosmin Ciausu,Marion Tonneau,Hugo Aerts,Raymond Mak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了零样本视觉语言模型（VLM）在非小细胞肺癌肿瘤勾画中的空间行为驱动因素。通过VoxTell模型的子提示分解、属性扰动、特异性阶梯和跨案例提示交换等分析，发现解剖位置是空间注意力的主导因素（63.4%的位置扰动导致灾难性下降），而组织学和分期影响较小。VoxTell在零样本下达到平均DSC 0.613，与微调模型nnUNet（0.690）无统计学显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本VLM为肿瘤勾画提供了可提示的替代方案，但控制其空间行为的提示维度尚不清楚。本文旨在探究哪些提示维度主导VLM的空间注意力行为。

Method: 对VoxTell模型进行子提示分解（诊断、人口学、分期、解剖、通用、无关控制）、属性扰动鲁棒性分析、特异性阶梯实验和跨案例提示交换，使用Dice相似系数（DSC）评估，采用Wilcoxon符号秩检验和Benjamini-Hochberg校正进行统计比较。

Result: 解剖位置是空间注意力的主导因素（63.4%位置扰动导致灾难性下降）；提示特异性从通用到完整描述提升性能，但仅诊断提示除外；无关提示正确产生零分割；跨案例交换确认患者特异性条件化（匹配DSC 0.906 vs 不匹配0.406）。组织学和分期替换影响极小。VoxTell零样本DSC为0.613，与nnUNet（0.690，校正p=0.156）和Ahmed等人（0.675，校正p=0.679）无显著差异，显著优于其他零样本模型。

Conclusion: 分割VLM不仅应通过Dice评估，还应通过其对齐的提示维度进行评估。模型优先关注"看哪里"而非"看什么"。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promptable alternative to task-specific training for gross tumor volume (GTV) delineation in non-small-cell lung cancer (NSCLC), but the prompt dimensions that govern their spatial behavior remain poorly understood. We study this question by probing alignment directions in VoxTell on a held-out internal NSCLC tumor dataset through sub-prompt decomposition into diagnosis, demographic, staging, anatomical, generic, and irrelevant controls; attribute-wise perturbation robustness; specificity ladders; and cross-case prompt swaps, while benchmarking against fine-tuned and zero-shot baselines using the Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC) with Wilcoxon signed-rank tests and Benjamini-Hochberg correction. Alignment analyses revealed that anatomical location is the dominant driver of VoxTell's spatial attention: 63.4 percent of location perturbations caused catastrophic drops, prompt specificity improved from generic to full descriptions except for diagnosis-only prompts, irrelevant prompts correctly yielded zero segmentation, and cross-case prompt swaps confirmed patient-specific conditioning (matched DSC 0.906 vs. mismatched 0.406). Histology and stage substitutions had minimal effect, indicating that the model prioritizes "where to look" over "what to look for." In this context, VoxTell, operating fully zero-shot, achieved a mean DSC of 0.613, statistically indistinguishable from nnUNet (0.690, adjusted p = 0.156) and Ahmed et al. (0.675, adjusted p = 0.679), while significantly outperforming all other zero-shot models. Together, these findings argue that segmentation VLMs should be evaluated not only by Dice, but also by the prompt dimensions to which they align.

</details>


### [34] [GameScope: A Multi-Attribute, Multi-Codec Benchmark Dataset for Gaming Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01272)
*Rajesh Sureddi,Shreshth Saini,Avinab Saha,Alan C. Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GameScope，是目前最大的游戏视频质量数据集，包含4,048个样本，涵盖H.264、H.265和AV1三种编解码器，以及UGC和PGC内容类型，每个样本由平均37人标注MOS评分和粗粒度质量属性。视觉语言模型在该数据集上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏视频流媒体质量评估数据集数量少且存在局限性，缺乏大规模、多样化、覆盖多种编解码器的数据集来支持跨编解码器一致工作的质量评估模型。

Method: 构建了包含4,048个视频样本的大规模数据集，覆盖H.264、H.265和AV1三种主流编解码器，包含UGC和PGC内容，每个样本由平均37名标注者提供MOS评分，并收集了粗粒度质量属性。

Result: 视觉语言模型在所有基准方法中表现最优；这是首个全面涵盖多种编解码器、内容类型及质量属性的游戏视频质量评估数据集。

Conclusion: GameScope填补了游戏视频质量评估领域的数据空白，为跨编解码器的质量评估研究提供了大规模、多样化的基准数据集，并验证了视觉语言模型在该任务上的优越性。

Abstract: The development of video game streaming has grown rapidly, with major platforms such as YouTube and Twitch using different codecs. To support quality assessment models that work consistently across any codec, it is necessary to have access to large, diverse subjective gaming quality datasets. Currently, there are only a few available, each having limitations. To address this gap, we present the largest gaming video quality dataset to date, incorporating both user-generated content (UGC) and professional-generated content (PGC) with extensive visual diversity. Our dataset covers the most widely used codecs - H.264, H.265, and AV1 - and consists of 4,048 video samples, each annotated by an average of 37 mean opinion score (MOS) ratings. In addition to overall quality scores, we collect coarse-grained quality attributes, enabling a better understanding of perceptual factors. We study the performance of leading video quality assessment methods on this dataset, including a vision language model that outperforms all the benchmarks. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first dataset that comprehensively addresses gaming video quality assessment across multiple codecs and content types with quality attributes. Our dataset is publicly available at https://rajeshsureddi.github.io/GameScope/.

</details>


### [35] [CNN-based Multi-In-Multi-Out Model for Efficient Spatiotemporal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01277)
*Hyeonseok Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CNN的Transformer架构模型MIMO-ESP，通过将时间轴作为独立轴处理并应用膨胀操作，在视频、交通和降水预测任务上实现了兼顾全局信息与高效复杂度的时空预测性能，超越了现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RNN模型存在并行化限制和递归误差累积问题；CNN模型难以捕获全局信息且将时间轴与通道轴混合导致信息混杂；Transformer模型复杂度高、训练时间长。需要一种能同时克服这些局限的高效时空预测模型。

Method: 提出MIMO-ESP模型，采用基于CNN的Transformer架构来考虑全局信息并提升复杂度；将时间轴作为独立轴处理，不与通道轴合并；应用膨胀操作（dilation）有效联合考虑时空信息。

Result: 在视频、交通和降水三个基准数据集上的实验表明，MIMO-ESP在保持竞争力的同时，性能超越了现有模型。消融研究验证了各组件模块的有效性。

Conclusion: MIMO-ESP通过CNN化的Transformer设计，在保持高效性的同时实现了高精度时空预测，为克服现有模型局限提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recently, Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) or Transformer architecture based models have been proposed to overcome the limitations of Recurrent Neural Network (RNN) based models in spatiotemporal prediction. These models prevent the inefficiency of parallelization limitation due to the sequential properties and stacked error due to the recursive method, and show high performance. Novertheless, there are still some challengies. First, CNN based models have difficulty considering global information due to the local properties of the kernel, and their performance is limited. In addition, information is mixed because the time axis is combined with the channel axis of the image for processing. Models based on Transformer architecture have high complexity due to the self-attention calcuation and take a long training time. In this paper, we propose a new structure model called CNN-based Multi-In-Multi-Out model for Efficient Spatiotemporal Prediction (MIMO-ESP) to overcome these limitations. MIMO-ESP considers global information and significantly improves complexity by configuring a Transformer architecture based on CNN. In addition, it treats the time axis as an independent axis without combining it, and effectively considers spatiotemporal information together by applying dilation. This structure makes MIMO-ESP efficient and high performance. Extensive experiment results on three promising benchmark datasets which including video, traffic, and precipitation prediction tasks demonstrate that the usefulness of MIMO-ESP due to the achieved competitive efficiency while outperforming existing models. Furthermore, the ablation study results demonstrate the usefulness of the components of MIMO-ESP, emphasizing the potential of the proposed approaches.

</details>

*David J. Richter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对植物叶片病害分类领域，系统评估了公开数据集和现有模型，构建了新数据集，并基于DenseNet201架构训练了基模型，该模型在迁移学习中表现更优、更快、更鲁棒，克服了数据不足等关键问题。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物病害每年造成巨大损失，早期发现和防治至关重要。传统人工检测耗时费力，虽然ML/CNN方法已取得进展，但公开数据集与训练完备模型所需之间存在显著差距，制约了领域发展。

Method: 1) 识别并评估现有公开植物叶片病害分类数据集和可训练模型，进行广泛基准测试；2) 基于评估结果和增强适用性研究，构建新数据集；3) 基于DenseNet201架构训练新基模型；4) 通过领域特定迁移学习实验验证模型性能。

Result: 新基模型在新数据集上优于基线模型，同时在植物叶片病害分类领域特定迁移学习实验中表现更优，能够更快、更鲁棒、更稳定地训练模型，且所需数据更少。

Conclusion: 新模型通过迁移学习有效克服了数据不足问题，训练速度更快、鲁棒性更强、稳定性更高，解决了该领域面临的关键挑战。

Abstract: Plants, crops and their yields are essential to our very existence, but diseases and pests cause large losses every year. As such it is vital to ensure that diseases can be spotted early and treated accordingly and stopping the spread while still possible. Manual and traditional methods require personal to walk through the field and check for symptoms 'by hand'. This is very laborious and very time consuming, so ML methods have been applied as a result and they have garnered promising results. CNN models are especially efficient as they can automatically extract features from images without any manual feature construction before then feeding the features to a classifier. Datasets are largely influential to the final performance of the model.
